# Rust → Go type mapping — LeeAttend apiserver

Phase 1 inventory. Source of truth: Rust declarations, serde attributes, Diesel schema, and OpenAPI component registration. Every citation uses `path:line`. `UNKNOWN — needs decision` marks unresolved wire/DB behavior, not an assumption.

## Inventory summary

- API-visible declarations inventoried: **548** (506 structs, 42 enums).
- OpenAPI `components.schemas` entries: **415**; resolved declaration sources: **415**; unresolved: **0**.
- Diesel model declarations: **136** across `apiserver/src/models/diesel/`; schema tables parsed: **127**.
- Float occurrences (`f32`/`f64`) under `apiserver/src`: **274** (MONEY **65**, TIME/DURATION **112**, OTHER **97**); all listed in [Float money/time audit](#float-moneytime-audit).
- Nullable/missing/empty and representation decisions are collected in [Ambiguity ledger](#ambiguity-ledger); ambiguity count: **12**.

## Rust wire rules

- `Option<T>` is nullable at Rust type level; omission on serialization depends on `skip_serializing_if` or struct-level behavior. Missing request fields generally deserialize to absent only when `Option` or `default` applies.
- `#[serde(default)]` makes missing input legal and supplies Rust default; it does not itself omit output.
- `#[serde(rename_all = ...)]`, field `rename`, `flatten`, `untagged`, `tag`, and aliases are copied verbatim into tables below.
- `Option<Option<T>>` plus `deserialize_option_option` is tri-state: missing, explicit `null`, or value. Preserve this in Go; do not collapse to one pointer.

### API/DB representation mismatches

- API UUIDs serialize as JSON strings; DB `Uuid` is PostgreSQL UUID. Go target: `uuid.UUID`, validate canonical text at boundary.
- API date/time fields use `chrono::DateTime<Utc>` / `NaiveDate`; DB schema distinguishes `Timestamptz` and `Date`. Go target must not use one generic string.
- API `serde_json::Value` maps to JSON object/value; DB `Jsonb` maps to `json.RawMessage` or validated typed struct at repository boundary.
- API money/leave amounts include `f64` in models and Diesel rows; this is a precision mismatch against PostgreSQL numeric/decimal fields where applicable. See float audit; Go target is `decimal.Decimal` for money.
- PostgreSQL arrays (`Array<...>`) map to Go slices; nullable element arrays (`Array<Nullable<T>>`) require `[]*T` or explicit nullable element wrapper, not `[]T`.
- Diesel update structs use nested `Option<Option<T>>` to distinguish omitted column from SQL NULL; ordinary API `Option<T>` cannot carry both states.
## Float money/time audit

- Total `f32`/`f64` occurrences under `apiserver/src`: **274** (MONEY **65**, TIME/DURATION **112**, OTHER **97**).
- Every occurrence is listed below with `path:line`; nothing is summarized away.
- Rust field declarations typed `f64` in API-visible models map to `decimal.Decimal` in this inventory (see DTO table); remaining occurrences are computations/tests and must be reviewed at their call site.

### DB float money columns (Float8)

These PostgreSQL columns store money/time values as `double precision` — a precision risk that must be resolved in the Go schema or explicitly preserved.

| Column | Schema declaration | Table | Go target |
|---|---|---|---|
| `base_amount -> Float8,` | `apiserver/src/schema.rs:1579` | `UNKNOWN` | `numeric`/`decimal.Decimal` (schema change) — UNKNOWN whether clients depend on float JSON: verify endpoint payloads before changing |
| `payout_amount -> Float8,` | `apiserver/src/schema.rs:1594` | `UNKNOWN` | `numeric`/`decimal.Decimal` (schema change) — UNKNOWN whether clients depend on float JSON: verify endpoint payloads before changing |
| `amount -> Float8,` | `apiserver/src/schema.rs:1649` | `UNKNOWN` | `numeric`/`decimal.Decimal` (schema change) — UNKNOWN whether clients depend on float JSON: verify endpoint payloads before changing |
| `standard_amount -> Float8,` | `apiserver/src/schema.rs:1652` | `UNKNOWN` | `numeric`/`decimal.Decimal` (schema change) — UNKNOWN whether clients depend on float JSON: verify endpoint payloads before changing |

## Ambiguity ledger

| # | Ambiguity | Evidence | Rule required for Go |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1|Nullable vs missing|`Option<T>` fields and nested `Option<Option<T>>` / `deserialize_option_option` in `apiserver/src/models/employee.rs:1074`, `apiserver/src/models/employment.rs:183`, `apiserver/src/models/custom_field.rs:433`|Pointers for nullable values; `Nullable[T]` (missing/null/value) for nested options; never turn missing into zero. |
| 2|Empty vs null vs absent arrays/strings|Serde defaults and optional vectors across DTOs; exact field attrs are in DTO table.|Preserve `null`, omitted, `[]`, and `""` separately at request boundary; only normalize where endpoint contract says so. |
| 3|Timestamp representation|`chrono::DateTime<Utc>` fields (e.g. `apiserver/src/models/time_tracking.rs:91`); `NaiveDate` fields (e.g. `apiserver/src/models/leave.rs:10`); Diesel `Timestamptz`/`Date` in `apiserver/src/schema.rs:...` table columns.|`time.Time` UTC for instants; date-only wrapper/UTC-midnight forbidden; parse RFC3339 with offset and normalize UTC. Location policy: workspace timezone only for calendar interpretation, never storage. |
| 4|`chrono`/`time` offset vs naive|Handlers use `DateTime<Utc>` and `NaiveDate`; no blanket offset policy in all DTOs.|Reject ambiguous local timestamps; require offset for instants; retain date-only values as date. |
| 5|Decimals vs floats|Money/leave fields use `f64` in `apiserver/src/models/payroll.rs:402`, `apiserver/src/models/leave.rs:276`, and Diesel `f64` in `apiserver/src/models/diesel/payroll.rs:23`; custom-field DB uses `Decimal` at `apiserver/src/models/diesel/custom_field.rs:40`.|Go money/rates: `decimal.Decimal`; DB numeric: `pgtype.Numeric` or decimal adapter; float JSON is legacy and must not be used for new money. |
| 6|UUIDs|Many DTO/entity fields use `Uuid`, e.g. `apiserver/src/models/employee.rs:789`; schema uses `Uuid`.|`uuid.UUID`; JSON canonical hyphenated string; reject malformed IDs. |
| 7|Binary/bytes|File download response and encrypted template paths include byte/string payloads, e.g. `apiserver/src/models/file.rs:235`, `apiserver/src/models/diesel/biometric_template.rs:20`.|Use `[]byte` only for actual binary; JSON base64 or URL must follow endpoint; do not expose encrypted material as arbitrary JSON. |
| 8|Arrays and nullable elements|`attachment_file_ids: Option<Vec<Option<Uuid>>>` at `apiserver/src/models/diesel/attendance_adjustment.rs:50`; schema arrays include nullable elements.|Use `[]*uuid.UUID` for nullable elements; outer pointer distinguishes SQL/API null from empty array. |
| 9|Tagged/untagged unions|`#[serde(untagged)]` at `apiserver/src/models/payroll.rs:499`; tagged payloads in queue/agent code are not all API components.|Decode with explicit discriminator where possible; for untagged union use ordered validation and preserve original JSON on unknown shape. |
| 10|Flattened objects|`#[serde(flatten)]` at `apiserver/src/models/contract.rs:132`, `apiserver/src/models/leave.rs:131`, `apiserver/src/handlers/monitor_alerts.rs:56`.|Go custom `UnmarshalJSON` or explicit merged struct; avoid nested field under flattened member. |
| 11|Enums DB vs JSON names|Diesel enum attrs in `apiserver/src/models/enums.rs:21`; JSON `rename_all` uses snake/SCREAMING/lowercase.|Typed Go string enums use JSON spelling; repository converts to PostgreSQL enum text/`pgtype.Enum`; never rely on Go identifier spelling. |
| 12|Pagination envelopes|Common `PaginatedResponse` at `apiserver/src/models/common.rs:192`; many `Paginated*` DTOs and `page`/`limit` query types in handlers.|Standardize only after endpoint contract comparison: preserve `data`, `total`, page/limit/cursor fields and integer widths; do not infer cursor semantics from page names. |

## Go target mapping

| Rust form | Go target | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| `Uuid` | `uuid.UUID` | JSON canonical string; DB UUID. |
| `String` | `string` | UTF-8; preserve empty vs absent via pointer. |
| `bool` | `bool` | Required unless Rust `Option`/default. |
| `i8/i16/i32/i64` | matching signed Go integer | Do not narrow DB/API integer; query page/limit validate bounds. |
| `u8/u16/u32/u64/usize` | matching unsigned/int | Use `int` only when wire width is not contractual. |
| `f32/f64` | `float32/float64` for measurements; `decimal.Decimal` for money/rates | All money/time occurrences audited above; no new money float. |
| `bigdecimal::BigDecimal` / `rust_decimal::Decimal` | `decimal.Decimal` or `pgtype.Numeric` | Decimal text/scale preserved at persistence boundary. |
| `chrono::DateTime<Utc>` / `time::OffsetDateTime` | `time.Time` | Require offset; normalize UTC; workspace zone only for display/calendar. |
| `chrono::NaiveDate` | date wrapper or `time.Time` date-only | Never treat as instant; JSON `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `Vec<T>` / `[T;N]` | `[]T` | Empty slice distinct from null pointer. |
| `Option<T>` | `*T` | Pointer means missing/null; custom presence tracking for PATCH. |
| `Option<Option<T>>` | `Nullable[T]` | Three states: missing, explicit null, value. |
| `serde_json::Value` | `json.RawMessage` or `any` | RawMessage at boundary; typed decode per endpoint. |
| `Vec<u8>` / bytes | `[]byte` | Base64 for JSON unless endpoint uses URL/string. |
| Serde enum | `type X string` | Constants use exact JSON name; repository maps DB enum. |
| Serde struct | named Go struct | Field JSON names/flatten/default rules copied from DTO table. |
| `HashMap<K,V>` | `map[K]V` | Validate JSON object key constraints. |
| `Result`/errors (not wire type) | HTTP error envelope | Use documented status/body; do not serialize Rust debug representation. |

### Per-type target rule

- Each API table struct maps to a named Go struct with JSON tags equal to its resolved serde JSON field name; field Go types come from the field cells.
- Each API table enum maps to a named Go string type with constants equal to the listed JSON names. DB-backed enums use repository conversion to PostgreSQL enum text/`pgtype` representation.
- Each OpenAPI component row maps to its resolved named Go type; unresolved rows are explicit `UNKNOWN — needs decision` and block implementation until declaration path is confirmed.
- Nullable fields use pointers; tri-state patch/update fields use a presence-aware `Nullable[T]` helper. This is one deliberate helper because standard pointers cannot distinguish missing from explicit null.

## Restart checklist

- Generate Go DTOs from this inventory only after OpenAPI registration and serde attributes are reviewed; field names are wire contract, Rust names are not.
- Add golden JSON tests for every enum family, flattened/untagged DTO, tri-state update field, date/time, decimal, UUID, array, and pagination envelope.
- Reject any new money/time `f32`/`f64`; add a ledger row and migration adapter if legacy endpoint cannot change immediately.
